- **find-SetBeamEndPos**: Locate SetBeamEndPos in CS2 server.dll / libserver.so via IDA Pro MCP and emit a fresh, minimal-unique signature or offset for the local gamedata entry "SetBeamEndPos" (symbol SetBeamEndPos). The gamedata consumer stores a BYTE SIGNATURE for this entry - ALWAYS emit the func schema (func_sig), never vfunc fields. - **find-skills**: Find an existing skill before writing one or working through a specialised task by hand.
